Complete register of regenerated paper meanings

Symbol gallery · Display adaptation review · Instructions

All 601 regenerated paper meanings, with the recorded reason and teaching scope. This register includes structural remakes as well as simplifications. Device-specific changes are separately recorded in each meaning’s display_fit entries in catalogue.json.

Completion report and priority inspection list

Ant insect

aac.ant-insect

swell-paper-v1 · index-embossed-v1 · viewplus-variable-height-v1 · monarch-display-v1 · dotpad-display-v1

Meaning-specific reconstruction with original 1.4 mm strokes and freshly sampled 1.2 mm Index dots. A one-node wingless worker-ant dorsal example retains a continuous head/neck/mesosoma/stalk/petiole/stalk/gaster contour, two eyes, two elbowed antennae, paired anterior mandibles and six jointed legs attached to the mesosoma. The head remains left; animal-left is displayed lower. Eyes, mandibular apertures and neck/stalk widths are enlarged independently for page size. No callout is present in the whole-ant meaning. This is one chosen worker form, not a universal ant species or node count; mesosoma includes thorax and propodeum.

Bee animal

aac.bee-animal

swell-paper-v1 · index-embossed-v1 · viewplus-variable-height-v1 · monarch-display-v1 · dotpad-display-v1

Meaning-specific reconstruction with original 1.4 mm strokes and freshly sampled 1.2 mm Index dots. The selected adult worker-bee plan retains the joined head, thorax and broad abdomen; two enlarged compound-eye outlines; two head antennae; six walking legs in three thorax-rooted pairs; and four thorax-rooted wing contours. The source triangular membrane convention remains, without invented venation, stripes, stinger or ant-like abdominal petiole. Appendage angles and eyes are independently enlarged or moved to maintain page-size separation. Two-segment legs are diagram carriers, not anatomical segment counts.

Boy young person

aac.boy-young-person

swell-paper-v1 · index-embossed-v1 · viewplus-variable-height-v1 · monarch-display-v1 · dotpad-display-v1

Meaning-specific reconstruction with original 1.4 mm strokes and freshly sampled 1.2 mm Index dots. The named fictional young-person example retains the source coat, mittens, trousers, boots, hair boundary and one lowered/one extended arm. The source left-right orientation is retained. The page geometry uses 70% source contour dimensions with unchanged 1.4 mm physical lines and newly placed Index dots; the hair cap is independently deepened to retain a usable closed region. No facial, bodily or clothing trait is asserted to identify gender. The boy/girl identity is supplied by the external character key, not inferred from the mirrored pose. No new fill-height meaning is added.

Butterfly animal

aac.butterfly-animal

swell-paper-v1 · index-embossed-v1 · viewplus-variable-height-v1 · monarch-display-v1 · dotpad-display-v1

Meaning-specific reconstruction with original 1.4 mm strokes and freshly sampled 1.2 mm Index dots. The dorsal spread-wing butterfly retains four wing regions, joined head/thorax/abdomen and two clubbed antennae. The narrow abdomen and antenna clubs are independently widened; wing attachment positions remain exact on the thorax contour. Legs remain occluded as in the source. Enlarged outlined clubs replace tiny solid dots. This is a generic monarch-informed recognition example, not complete species or sex identification.

Cat animal

aac.cat-animal

swell-paper-v1 · index-embossed-v1 · viewplus-variable-height-v1 · monarch-display-v1 · dotpad-display-v1

Meaning-specific reconstruction with original 1.4 mm strokes and freshly sampled 1.2 mm Index dots. The side-view cat retains pointed ears, a raised curved tail, four separately readable legs and one near-eye marker. Legs, feet and tail thickness are re-laid out for page-size touch. The small muzzle fold is omitted; the eye marker is enlarged and outlined rather than a tiny solid dot. This is a generic recognition outline, not a breed or anatomical scale model.

Cow animal

No horns or udder absent from the source are invented.

Doctor medical role

aac.doctor-medical-role

swell-paper-v1 · index-embossed-v1 · viewplus-variable-height-v1 · monarch-display-v1 · dotpad-display-v1

Meaning-specific reconstruction with original 1.4 mm strokes and freshly sampled 1.2 mm Index dots. The complete source human/equipment composition is rebuilt with fixed physical 1.4 mm strokes and freshly placed Index dots. The stethoscope yoke is shortened vertically and its tube lengthened, with chestpiece moved 1 source mm upwards to retain body clearance. Clothing, hand, skin/hair and footwear domains are retained. Hair caps, where present, are independently deepened. ViewPlus retains the original #555555 line-height role without invented shaded areas. This is a keyed role/context example, not an assertion that a generic figure or isolated tool universally identifies an occupation or action.

Hairbrush object

aac.hairbrush-object

swell-paper-v1 · index-embossed-v1 · viewplus-variable-height-v1 · monarch-display-v1 · dotpad-display-v1

Meaning-specific reconstruction with original 1.4 mm strokes and freshly sampled 1.2 mm Index dots. The horizontal brush retains six representative bristle projections, an elongated rounded head and attached right handle. The six former very thin outlined bristles are deliberately widened to 8 mm and separated by 6 mm centreline gaps; this keeps the material bodies open and traceable. Head and handle are independently widened and their join remains continuous. Bristle count is a selected recognition cue, not a literal product specification. ViewPlus grey stays within the single material silhouette.

Keyboard device

aac.keyboard-device

swell-paper-v1 · index-embossed-v1 · viewplus-variable-height-v1 · monarch-display-v1 · dotpad-display-v1

Keys are re-laid out as 10 by 8.2 mm outlines with at least 3.1 mm clear gaps. Acoustic guitar object example

swell-paper-v1 · index-embossed-v1 · viewplus-variable-height-v1 · monarch-display-v1 · dotpad-display-v1

Meaning-specific reconstruction with original 1.4 mm strokes and freshly sampled 1.2 mm Index dots. The neck is greatly widened and the body shortened independently to preserve all six 8 mm-spaced strings and 9 mm tuning-button glyphs. Strings cross the white soundhole and end at the bridge; this is a tactile teaching layout, not realistic instrument proportions or a maker replica.

Actor role context

edu.actor-role-context

swell-paper-v1 · index-embossed-v1 · viewplus-variable-height-v1 · monarch-display-v1 · dotpad-display-v1

Meaning-specific reconstruction with original 1.4 mm strokes and freshly sampled 1.2 mm Index dots. The actor remains within a separately resized stage header, two side curtain bands and a shared floor. The source tunic, extended hand, lowered hand, head/neck, two exposed legs and two shoes remain. The figure contours are independently rebuilt at 58% source coordinate dimensions with original 1.4 mm strokes; the stage is narrowed independently instead of shrinking the person with it. The lowered hand and forearm are moved 2 mm outward, with the left curtain moved outward to preserve clearance. Shoes keep their actual floor contacts. The theatrical setting plus external role key supplies context; generic clothing or gesture alone does not identify an actor.

Adult mandibular dental arch

edu.adult-mandibular-dental-arch

swell-paper-v1 · index-embossed-v1 · viewplus-variable-height-v1 · monarch-display-v1 · dotpad-display-v1

Meaning-specific reconstruction with original 1.4 mm strokes and freshly sampled 1.2 mm Index dots. The selected complete adult lower exploded arch retains all sixteen crown outlines, including third molars: per side two incisors, one canine, two premolars and three molars. Source crown class outlines and patient-right on page-left are retained. The lower arch has incisors at the bottom and posterior teeth at the top. Incisors are enlarged independently and gaps expanded; this is an illustrative class/position inventory, not a clinical shape, root or cusp model and not universal adult dentition.

Alaska flag layout context

edu.alaska-flag-layout-context

swell-paper-v1 · index-embossed-v1 · viewplus-variable-height-v1

Reconstructed the retained flag layout with original 1.4 mm strokes and fresh 1.2 mm Index dots. Seven smaller five-point Big Dipper stars and one larger Polaris star remain, without connecting lines. The complete relative star-centre arrangement is retained with a uniform 1.05 coordinate factor; small stars are enlarged to 10.2 mm radius and Polaris keeps the 10:7 size ratio. The surrounding field changes from 240:165 to 194:146 to remove excess margins. This is an explicit accessible layout adaptation, not cloth proportions or an astronomical chart. The page-fit artwork is rotated clockwise: the hoist is at the top. Turn the page anticlockwise for the conventional horizontal reading orientation.

Numeric Celsius tick labels are not supplied in the retained source and must be explicitly introduced in the external key; these are synthetic teaching values, not observed weather or climate. Physical ticks are lengthened to 13 mm.

Meaning-specific reconstruction with original 1.4 mm strokes and freshly sampled 1.2 mm Index dots. Whale forelimb skeleton model

edu.whale-forelimb-skeleton-model

Meaning-specific reconstruction with original 1.4 mm strokes and freshly sampled 1.2 mm Index dots. The introduced humpback whale regional forelimb retains one humerus, separate radius and ulna, one grouped wrist, and four unsegmented digital-ray regions II-V. The two central ray regions remain longer than the lateral ones; no digit I is added. At page size all bone/region polygons are widened outward by 1.3 mm after relaying their source positions; the grouped wrist is shifted 1 mm distally to preserve its gaps. Fine contour proportions change; the rays deliberately do not enumerate metacarpals or phalanges and must not be interpreted as single biological bones. No flipper soft-tissue outline, anatomical specimen length or generic all-whale pattern is claimed.
